But the explicit encouragement: do something different than above. Maybe implement LP-based SOTA from planning: maximize sum g under f*g≤M. That could be powerful and different. Let's examine LP update in detail.

Given f normalized sum maybe 1. M = max conv(f,f)? Or constraint for f*g where g variable. They say Starting from f normalized sum √(2n), set M=||f*f||∞. Next compute g0 by solving LP maximizing ∑ b_j subject to b_j≥0 and ||f*g0||∞≤M; as standard, optimum at extreme point... Rescale g0 to match normalization, update f←(1-t)f+tg. Repeating produces nonincreasing ||f*f||∞.

This looks like a convex optimization step: Given f, M=current max self-conv. The set K = {g≥0: f*g ≤ M} is convex polytope. The point g maximizing sum (i.e. in direction one) has same sum as f? They rescale. Then blend. This may push f towards a point where self-conv bound lower. Maybe equivalent to finding best response in a zero-sum game? Could be related to finding minimax distribution for matrix game: minimize max_j a^TQ_j a. The gradient of max_j is active weights. LP maybe gives supergradient descent in a different norm? Need implement?

LP dimension: variables g length n, constraints length 2n-1. For n up to 8192, LP huge; CVXPY with CLP may be too slow. But due to special convolution structure, maybe can solve dual via min-cost covering with shifted nonnegative kernels. Dual: minimize M ∑ y_k subject to (y * f?)_i ≥1, y≥0. Let's get exact signs.

Dual: min M 1^T y s.t. A^T y ≥ 1, y≥0. A^T y at i = ∑_{k} A_{k,i} y_k = ∑_{k} f_{k-i} y_k. Let l=k-i; as k=0..2n-2, l= -i..2n-2-i, require 0≤l<n. So term = ∑_{l=0}^{n-1} f_l y_{i+l}. Let's refine log LSE objective:
```
def lse_log_func(x, beta):
    x = np.asarray(x)
    x = x - x.mean()
    # optional clip x to [-25,25]? If use bounds, not needed; but line search may go out; we can clip for value and gradient? Clipping makes nonsmooth; but bounds in optimizer handle.
    m = x - x.max()
    v = np.exp(m)
    p = v / v.sum()
    c = fast_self_conv(p)
    d = n*c
    dmax = c.max()*n
    if dmax <=0: return inf, gradzero
    z = beta*(d-dmax); z -= max? dmax subtract already. e=np.exp(z); sum_e; w=e/sum_e
    L = dmax + log(sum_e)/beta
    h = corr_pair(p,w)
    wdotc = dot(w,c)
    grad_p = 4*n*(h - wdotc)
    grad_p = nan_to_num
    grad_x = p*grad_p
    grad_x -= grad_x.mean()
    # scale objective by n? return float(n*(2*L + penalty?)), grad_x * n
```

```
def lse_log_eval(x, beta):
   x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float64)
   x = np.clip(x, -30, 30) # if no bounds? maybe not if scipy bounds.
   x = x - x.mean()
   z = x - x.max(); p = exp(z); p /= p.sum()
   n=...
   c=fast_self_conv(p)
   dmax=n*c.max()
   if dmax<=0 return inf, zero
   # Since d = n*c, beta*d - beta*dmax = beta*n*(c-cmax)
   beta2 = beta*n
   zc = beta2*(c-cmax); e=exp(zc-cmax? )
   sum_e; w=e/sum_e
   L = dmax + log(sum_e)/beta  # careful beta original for d
   h=corr_pair(p,w)
   lds_c = dot(w,c)
   grad_p = 4*n*(h - lds_c)
   grad_x = p*grad_p
   grad_x -= grad_x.mean()
   # scale by n
   val = n*(2*L)
   grad_x *= n
   return val, grad_x
```

Objective:
```
def fun_log(x,beta,n):
   x = np.asarray(x)
   # optional clip to [-30,30]
   x = np.clip(x, -25,25)
   z=x-x.max(); v=exp(z); p=v/v.sum()
   c=fast_self_conv(p)
   cmax=c.max()
   if cmax<=0: return inf
   beff = beta*float(n)
   zc=beff*(c-cmax)
   # want normalize with max: exp(zc) since max term 1
   e=np.exp(zc); sum_e=e.sum()
   w=e/sum_e
   L = float(n)*cmax + np.log(sum_e)/beta  # because dmax=n*cmax; L=dmax + log(sum_e)/beta
   h=corr_pair(p,w)
   lds = dot(w,c)
   grad_p = 4*n*(h - lds)
   grad_x = p*grad_p
   grad_x -= grad_x.mean()
   val = n*(2*L) + 1e-8*float(np.dot(x,x)) # or mean pen
   grad_x = n*grad_x + 1e-8*2*x
   return val, grad_x
```

If beta small, beff small; w near uniform, L≈mean d = n*(sum c)/(2n-1)=n/(2n-1)≈0.5; F≈n. Exact score≈1.5. okay.

Implement `lse_log_fn`:
```
def lse_log_fn(x, beta, n, reg):
    x = np.nan_to_num(np.asarray(x,float), nan=0.0, posinf=50.0, neginf=-50.0)
    xm = x - np.mean(x)
    # optional large clip
    if np.max(np.abs(xm)) > 50: xm = np.clip(xm, -50,50)
    z = xm - np.max(xm)
    z = np.clip(z, -745.0, 0.0)
    v = np.exp(z); p=v/v.sum()
    c=fast_self_conv(p)
    cmax=float(np.max(c))
    if cmax <=1e-300: return 1e100, zeros
    beff = float(beta)*float(n)
    # to avoid overflow, beff*(c-cmax) <=0; but if cmax zero no.
    zc = beff*(c-cmax)
    zc = np.clip(zc, -745.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(zc)
    sum_e = float(e.sum())
    if sum_e<=0 or not finite: fallback uniform active? 
    w=e/sum_e
    L = float(n)*cmax + np.log(sum_e)/beta
    h=corr_pair(p,w)
    lds = float(np.dot(w,c))
    grad_p = 4.0*float(n)*(h - lds)
    grad_p = np.nan_to_num(...)
    grad_x = p*grad_p
    grad_x -= grad_x.mean()
    val = float(n)*(2.0*L) + reg*float(np.dot(xm,xm))
    grad_x = grad_x*float(n) + 2.0*reg*xm
    # if val inf return zero? regularization helps.
    return val, grad_x
```
Scaling: grad_x scaled by n. `val` includes reg not scaled? Should be consistent: reg term value not scaled by n? It's small; gradient 2reg*xm. Since objective scaled by n for main, reg should maybe scaled too? Doesn't matter. But line search uses gradient scaled by n; reg gradient not scaled, negligible. Could set reg_scaled = 1e-4 * n? That might be too large. Keep reg=1e-8*n? Hmm. To keep true to scaled objective, main part n*2L (O1e4), reg part n*1e-6*dot? Use `reg=1e-6`, val += n*reg*dot(xm,xm), grad += n*2*reg*xm. That gives reg gradient O(n*1e-6*1)=0.03 for n=32768, okay. Value main 1.5n=49152, reg 0.03 per? dot~n? 32768*? Actually dot(xm,xm)~n*var~32768, n*reg*dot=32768*1e-6*32768=1075, too large if var1? Wait dot ~n (if x deviations ~1), val reg= n*reg*dot =32768*1e-6*32768=1075, not negligible but less than main 49152; maybe ok. reg=1e-8 ->10.75. Use reg=1e-8. Gradient n*2reg*x ~2e-8*32768*1=0.00065, tiny. Maybe too tiny to prevent drift. But gradient main O1; drift not issue. Use reg=1e-10.
Maybe skip reg and just mean subtract in x before softmax. But then x mean not fixed for line search? If function uses xm=x-mean(x) and gradient projected mean-zero, it is smooth; no flat. No bounds. We can add tiny reg on xm to handle large. Let's use no bounds, xm internal, grad mean zero. For safety, clip xm to [-50,50]. This non-smooth but rare. Add reg=1e-12.

If `sum_e` underflows? e max term 1, so sum>=1. Good if zc max 0. But due float c-cmax maybe positive tiny for max? clip upper0. sum>=1.
`corr_pair` could be expensive; if beta high and w sparse, we can sparse. Let's implement `compute_corr(p,w,method)`.
Maybe in `lse_log_fn`, if `np.sum(np.exp(zc - np.max(zc))) < ...`? We can determine active K if beta large: find top 64 indices of c, set w_sparse = top weights (softmax restricted) and compute h by loops. But L-BFGS with piecewise smooth top set each eval might be okay if set changes. Dense LSE smoother. Keep dense.

### Progressive optimization schedule in propose_candidate
Pseudo:
```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400,**kw):
 t0=monotonic(); deadline=t0+budget-60
 rng=...
 # get parent
 parent = get_parent()
 # Base length selection: if parent length ~6962, use 8192,16384,32768
 # Start with n0 = 8192 (or parent_len if between 4096 and16384)
 all_best = parent if parent else None
 # Stage 1: n=4096 maybe quick to refine shape (if parent available)
 n1=4096
 a1=init_best(n1, parent)
 sc,a1=optimize_log(a1, deadline=t0+200, ...)
 update best
 # Stage 2: n=8192
 a=resample(best,8192)
 sc,a=optimize_log(a, t0+700, ...)
 # Stage 3: n=16384
 a=resample(best,16384)
 sc,a=optimize_log(a, t0+1700,...)
 # Stage 4: if time, n=32768 but only if best score <1.51 and time
 a=resample(best,32768)
 sc,a=optimize_log(a, t0+deadline-100,...)
 # Final polish: active pressure, exact subgradient, small direct LSE at 20000 beta.
 # Return best.
```
Need not hardcode absolute times if budget different. Use fractions of total. Budget 2400. Define `main_end = deadline - 120`. Fractions:

#### fast_self_conv
```
def fast_self_conv(a):
 a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
 n=a.size
 if n==0: return empty
 M=2*n-1
 N=1<<(M-1).bit_length()
 A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
 out=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:M]
 out=np.maximum(out,0)
 return out
```
Small negative due FFT; max0. For score, okay.

#### corr_pair
Previous:
```
def corr_pair(a,w):
 a=np.asarray(a,float); w=...
 n=a.size; m=w.size
 M=n+m-1; N=...
 A=np.fft.rfft(a[::-1],N)
 W=np.fft.rfft(w,N)
 full=np.fft.irfft(A*W,N)[:M]
 return full[n-1:2*n-1]
```
Check orientation with grad. We can test small n=2 p=[a,b], w=[w0,w1,w2]. h_i=∑_k w_k p_{k-i}, valid.
For i=0: h0=w0 p0 + w1 p1? k=0,1,2: p_k? p_{0}=p0, p1, p2 invalid. h0=w0 p0 + w1 p1.
For i=1: h1=w1 p0? k-i=0 =>k=1, w1 p0; w2 p1. h1=w1 p0 + w2 p1.
Now corr_pair via a[::-1]=[b,a], w=[w0,w1,w2]. conv length 4? full:
k0: b*w0
k1: b*w1 + a*w0
k2: b*w2 + a*w1
k3: a*w2
M=2+3-1=4, slice [n-1:2n-1]=[1:3] => [b*w1 + a*w0, b*w2 + a*w1] = [w1 a + w0 b? w0 b? Wait h0 expected w0 p0 + w1 p1 = w0 a + w1 b. Got a*w0 + b*w1 yes same. h1 expected w1 p0 + w2 p1 = w1 a + w2 b. Got b*w2 + a*w1. Good.
So corr_pair correct.

def estimate_eval_log(n):
 N1=next_power_of_two(2*n-1)
 N2=next_power_of_two(3*n-2)
 return 0.00025*np.log2(N1) + 0.0006*np.log2(N2) + 0.01
```

`softmax_from_x(x)`:
```
def softmax_from_x(x):
 x=np.nan_to_num(...)
 x=x-np.mean(x)
 # if all near same, p uniform
 z=x-np.max(x)
 # if max-min >700, clip z min -700
 z=np.clip(z, -745, 0)
 v=np.exp(z); s=v.sum(); if s<... uniform
 p=v/s
 return p
```
But if x has large constant, mean subtract.

### Final exact polish
`subgrad_polish(a, max_iters, deadline)` as previous but use top K and line search. We'll improve:
```
def subgrad_polish(a, max_iters=30, deadline=None):
 a=normalize_a(a); best=a; best_sc=exact; cur=best; cur_sc=best_sc
 for it in range(max_iters):
   c,_ = d_from_a? We need c and S. Use `c=fast_self_conv(a); S=sum(a)
   cmax=c.max()
   if time break
   moved=False
   for k in (1,2,4,8,16):
      w=active_weights(c,k)
      h=corr_pair(a,w)
      hmax=h.max()
      if hmax<eps continue
      for mult in (0.005,0.02,0.06,0.2,0.5):
         eta=mult/hmax
         q=a*np.exp(-eta*h)
         q=normalize_a(q)
         sc=exact_score_a(q)
         if sc<best_sc: best...
         if sc<cur_sc: cur...
      # line search maybe binary? Keep.
   if cur is None break
   a=cur
   if no improvement for 3 iterations break
```
But this evaluates many exact convolutions: K*s ~20 per iter; each exact FFT self only O(2n). For n=32768, 20*60=1200 FFT self, maybe heavy. Limit iterations and n. Use max_iters based on n: 10 if n>16384 else 20. K (1,3,8), mult (0.02,0.1,0.3) =>9 evals per iter; 10=>90 FFT, okay. Use deadline check.
Could use `c` already computed; for q exact uses fast_self_conv again.

`active_weights(c,k)`: use argpartition, w ones/k on top. If k=1, top only max. Note max coefficient may be one; top K smooths.

### Choosing final length and schedule in propose_candidate
Let's write more concretely.

```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
 t0=time.monotonic(); total=float(budget_s); deadline=t0+total-60

```
Time fractions: Let total usable `T = deadline - t0`.
- If parent none, first n_small: 0.10T; n_mid: 0.35T; n_large: 0.45T; final:0.10.
- If parent available: skip n_small? Maybe parent refined already; use n_mid=8192 for 0.20T, n_large=16384 for0.40T, n_xlarge=32768 for0.30T, final0.10.
We want maximize final large. Could do:
   t1 = t0 + 0.15*usable (8192 direct/log)
   t2 = t0 + 0.55*usable (16384)
   t3 = deadline - 120 (32768)
But if parent none, 32768 from random may not be good; use 16384 large.
Simplify:
- Always run a short init/scoring at n_init = 8192 (or 4096 if no parent). This can provide a shape.
- Then main stages:
   Stage A length 8192: time min(180s, 0.12*usable) if parent else 0.15.
   Stage B length 16384: time 0.42*usable.
   Stage C length 32768: remaining before final (if time>300).
Final 120s.

But if no parent, optimizing 16384 directly from random might be hard. We can include stage A 4096 for 200s to find shape, then 8192 300s, then 16384 rest. That's safer. Let's implement dynamic based on parent.

### Testing gradient signs
Our direct gradient previous from LSE. For log gradient, scale. Could there be a sign error? Minimizing score; gradient of F should point increase. We use grad_x = p*grad_p with grad_p positive where increasing? For single active, grad_p=4n(p_{k-i}-c_k). If p on active diagonal high? Moving p along projected negative gradient should reduce active. L-BFGS minimization uses negative. Sign seems consistent. Let's test n=2 a=[1/3,2/3] score1.777 vs uniform2. For optimum a increasing low->high? Variables p0=1/3,p1=2/3. Objective constraints: c0=p0^2=0.111, c1=2p0p1=0.444, c2=0.444. Max c1,c2. Gradient for active c2 (k=2 single): h_i=2*p_{2-i}? h0=2p2? invalid? Wait corr w at k=2: h0=p2 invalid +? Our corr w at k=2: h0? w2 p_{2}? invalid? For i=0, k-i=2 invalid ->h0=0; for i=1, k-i=1 ->h1=p1. But derivative of c2=p1^2 is 2p1 for p1 only. Our formula derivative 2p_{k-i}: for i0 none, i1 p1 ->2? Wait corr h_i=∑w p_{k-i}; for k=2, i0 p2 invalid 0, i1 p1 -> h0? Actually i=1 h1=p1. The derivative factor 2? We omitted in grad_p? We used grad_p=4n(h - LDS_c). For single active, h_i = p_{k-i} derivative/2? Yes derivative c=2h. grad_p =2 * derivative d? F=2n c => 2n*2h=4n h; minus normalization 2n c? Wait grad_p formula earlier 4n(h - LDS_c). For c2: h=[0,p1], LDS_c=c2=p1^2. grad_p0=4n(0-p1^2) negative; grad_p1=4n(p1-p1^2) positive. For n=2,p1=2/3: grad=[-4*2*4/9=-96/9? =-10.667, 8*(2/3-4/9)=8*2/9=1.777]. Dot p =0.333*(-10.667)+0.667*1.777=0. Negative gradient direction increases p0 and decreases p1, moving toward uniform? But optimum is p0<p1; current at optimum should gradient on simplex zero for active constraints c1 and c2 both active (c1=0.444,c2=0.444). If beta high w on both k=1,2. w uniform? Active weights maybe. For both, h: k=1 h=[p1,p0], k=2 h=[0,p1]; average h=[p1/2,p0/2+p1/2]; LDS_c=(c1+c2)/2=0.444. grad=4n(h-0.444): p0:4*2*(1/3-0.444)=-1.333; p1:8*(2/3-0.444)=1.778; not zero. But exact active set c1,c2 both max, subgradient is convex combination; there may be a combination λ making gradient zero? Let w=λ at k1, 1-λ at k2. h0=λ p1 + (1-λ)*0 =λ/3; h1=λ p0 +(1-λ)p1=λ/3+(1-λ)2/3. LDS=0.444. Need h0=0.444? λ/3=4/9 =>λ=4/3 impossible. So no combination gives zero? Could there be lower n=2? We solved min max of three quadratics; at x=1/3, c1=c2=4/9; c0=1/9. Increase x decrease c1? Let x=0.4: c1=0.48,c2=0.36 max0.48 worse. x=0.333 max0.444. Seems optimum.  if S<1e-8 return 1e100, zeros

 h=corr_pair(a,w)

#### `optimize_sequence` detailed
```
def optimize_sequence(a0, deadline, rng, label, kind):
    a=normalize_a(a0)
    if a.size==0: return inf,a
    best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=exact_score_a(a)
    n=len(a)
    t_start=time.monotonic()
    total=max(5.0, float(deadline - t_start))
    print(label start ...)
    # schedule betas/fractions
    schedule = [(20,0.05),(60,0.06),(150,0.08),(400,0.10),(1000,0.12),(2500,0.14),(6000,0.14),(15000,0.13),(40000,0.18)]
    cum=0.0
    for beta,frac in schedule:
       sub_dead = t_start + (cum+frac)*total
       sub_dead = min(sub_dead, float(deadline)-3.0)
       cum += frac
      _abort if time > sub_dead-6
       attempts=0
       while time < sub_dead - 6 and attempts < 4:
          rem=float(sub_dead - time - 2)
          if rem < 8: break
          mf=estimate_maxfun(rem*0.8,n)
          if mf<50: break
          if kind == 'direct':
             sc,a_run=run_lbfgs_direct(a,beta,deadline,mf)
          else:
             sc,a_run=run_lbfgs_log_from_a(a,beta,deadline,mf)
          attempts +=1
          if sc < best_sc:
             best_sc=sc; best_a=a_run.copy(); print
             a=a_run.copy()
          else:
             if sc < best_sc + 0.005:
                 a=a_run.copy()
             # else keep a
          # if no improvement maybe break? if attempts>1 break
          if sc >= best_sc + 0.01 and attempts>0: break
    if time < deadline - 20:
       # final high beta within this run? schedule already high.
    return best_sc,best_a
```
But note: If beta schedule fractions total 1, sub_dead for later may be before time if previous ran long. The while loop uses current a. If previous overshoots sub_dead, later skipped. Good.
Number of attempts per beta 4; if maxfun small, multiple runs. If maxfun large, one. Maxfun estimate uses rem and cap 2500; for 30s rem at n16384 est0.08 ->375; okay.
For log kind, `run_lbfgs_log_from_a` re-logs from current a (which is normalized). Good.
Potential issue: kind direct and log use different objective scaling; exact scores comparable.

`estimate_maxfun`:
```
def estimate_maxfun(t_left,n):
 t_left=max(1.0,float(t_left))
 if t_left<5: return 64
 est=estimate_eval_time(n)
 mf=int((0.75*t_left)/max(est,1e-4))
 return max(64,min(2500,mf))
```
`estimate_eval_time` as direct/log similar; for log maybe higher due corr. We'll use same.
For n=4096 estimate 0.006? Use formula:
```
def estimate_eval_time(n):
 logn=max(1.0,np.log2(max(16,n)))
 scale=max(0.5, float(n)/8192.0)
 return 0.03*scale*max(1.0, logn/np.log2(8192.0))
```
n4096 scale0.5 ->0.015*0.84=0.0126. n8192 0.03. n16384 0.06*1.06=0.0636. n32768 0.12*1.11=0.133. Good.

def generate_inits(n,rng,parent,cap=None):

 x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
 if parent is not None:
   pr=resample_sequence(parent,n); add(pr); add(pr[::-1])
 add(ones)
 for alpha in (0.2,0.5,0.8): add(1+alpha*(2x-1)); add(1-alpha*(2x-1))
 # power skew
 for pwr in (0.5,2,3): add(eps + x**pwr); add(eps+(1-x)**pwr)
 # trig

 for r in range(max_trig): ...

 max_low=4 if n>16384 else6
 Nf=next_power_of_two(n)
 ...
 # blocks
 for nb in (8,16,32): ...
 # random
 add eps+random
 return inits

### Potential performance issue: optimize_sequence schedule always full 9 betas even for short sub_dead. If sub_dead short, fractions small but still loop maybe one beta. The first beta sub_dead= t0 + 0.05*total_stage; if total_stage 360, sub_dead=18s after start; good. It will attempt beta20 for 18s, then beta60 next 0.06*360=21.6s, etc. Good.
If time already close to sub_dead due previous stage overshoot, skip.
Within a beta, while attempts<4. If maxfun estimate huge (e.g., rem 100s, est0.06 ->1250), one run 100s maybe overshoot? `run_lbfgs` doesn't check deadline during minimize except maxfun; maxfun estimated for rem*0.7, but line search overhead maybe. It might overrun sub_dead. We pass `deadline=stage_deadline` to run but it only uses to cap maxfun before start; no interrupt during minimize. If overruns, next stages skipped. Acceptable. Could reduce maxfun by 0.5 factor.

`run_lbfgs_*` sets maxfun cap by `(0.9*rem)/est`. We can pass sub_dead as deadline, so it uses rem to sub_dead. Good. The `minimize` won't stop at sub_dead but maxfun chosen to finish before. Overrun possible; okay.

### Correctness of LSE value in log and direct
Direct `score = 2*L + pen*(S-1)^2`, where L = dmax + log(sum_e)/beta. This approximates 2*max d. d = n*c/S^2. exact=2*max d. Good.
Log main value `n*(2*L)` because L uses p normalized S=1. Scaling grad by n. `val` also includes reg; not exactly n times reg, but negligible. For consistency in line search, gradient main scaled by n. reg gradient should maybe not scaled; if val reg not scaled, gradient 2*reg*x. Since reg tiny. Fine.
Potential issue: If `beta` is high and `sum_e` ~1, L=dmax exactly. LSE gradient with w on max coeff; if multiple cmax equal, argpartition? e gives equal. Good.

### Potential issue in log: p from softmax is strictly positive; optimal may have zeros. At final output, we can set very small values to 0? But if we zero and renormalize, score might increase slightly. Maybe keep positive tiny. The problem allows zero; no need. If values underflow to zero in softmax, score computed with zeros; okay. If output contains 0.0, valid. We can clip negative to0.

### More ideas for improvement
Could use exact active constraints in log objective: instead of LSE over all coefficients, use LSE of top K plus small penalty? At high beta, LSE already. But computing dense w via exp over 3n coefficients costs O(n) small.
Could use `active_weights` for final high beta to reduce corr cost? Maybe implement in `run_lbfgs_log_from_a` an option `sparse=False`; not necessary.

Could add `parent` resampling with anti-aliased smoothing? Linear interp. If parent has oscillations, upsample loses high freq; optimizer can reintroduce.

#### Function definitions detail:

`next_power_of_two`:
```
def next_power_of_two(m):
    m = int(max(1,m))
    return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()
```

`fast_self_conv`:
```
def fast_self_conv(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0: return np.empty(0,dtype=np.float64)
    M=2*n-1; N=next_power_of_two(M)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    out=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:M]
    out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    return out
```
Tiny negative ignored. But if significant negative due precision? no.

`corr_pair`:
```
def corr_pair(a,w):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel(); w=...
    n=a.size; m=w.size
    if n==0 or m==0: return empty
    M=n+m-1; N=...
    A=np.fft.rfft(a[::-1],N)
    W=np.fft.rfft(w,N)
    full=np.fft.irfft(A*W,N)[:M]
    out=full[n-1:2*n-1]
    return np.nan_to_num(out, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
```

`normalize_a`:
```
def normalize_a(a,target=1.0):
 a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
 if a.size==0: return empty
 a=np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
 a=np.clip(a,0.0,None)
 S=float(a.sum())
 if S < 1e-12: return np.full(a.size,target/a.size)
 a = a * (target/S)
 S2=float(a.sum())
 if S2<1e-12: return full
 return a*(target/S2)
```

`resample_sequence`:
```
def resample_sequence(a,n):
 a=...; m=a.size; n=int(n)
 if m==0: return ones(n)/n
 if m==n: return a.copy()
 x_old=np.linspace(0,1,m); x_new=np.linspace(0,1,n)
 out=np.interp(x_new,x_old,a)
 return np.maximum(out,0.0)
```
Normalize outside.

`exact_score_a`:
```
 def exact_score_a(a):
  a=np.asarray...; n; S=sum
  if S<0.01: return np.inf
  c=fast_self_conv(a); cmax=float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0
  if cmax<0: cmax=0
  return float(2*n*cmax/(S*S))
```
If S small; internal sum1.

`d_from_a`:
```
def d_from_a(a):
 a=...; n; S=sum
 if S<1e-12: return zeros(2n-1),S
 c=fast_self_conv(a)
 d=(float(n)*c)/(S*S)
 d=np.maximum(d,0.0)
 return d,S
```

`lse_direct_fn`:
```
def lse_direct_fn(a,beta,pen):
    a=np.asarray(a,np.float64).ravel()
    n=a.size
    grad=np.zeros(n)
    a=np.nan_to_num(a,nan=0.0,posinf=1e3,neginf=0.0)
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    S=float(a.sum())
    if S < 1e-8:
        return 1e100, grad
    c=fast_self_conv(a)
    d=(float(n)*c)/(S*S)
    dmax=float(np.max(d))
    if not np.isfinite(dmax) or dmax <=0:
        return 1e100, grad
    z=beta*(d-dmax)
    z=np.clip(z,-745,0)
    e=np.exp(z); sum_e=float(e.sum()); if sum_e<=0... 
    w=e/sum_e
    h=corr_pair(a,w)
    LDS=float(np.dot(w,d))
    score=2.0*(dmax + np.log(sum_e)/beta) + pen*(S-1.0)*(S-1.0)
    grad=(4.0*float(n)*h)/(S*S) - (4.0*LDS)/S + 2.0*pen*(S-1.0)
    grad=np.nan_to_num(grad,...); grad=np.clip(grad,-1e6,1e6)
    return float(score), grad
```
Check gradient formula signs. Good.
If `beta` small, log(sum_e) okay. Beta might be 0? schedule >0. If `beta` not finite, set 100.
Add `beta=float(beta); if beta<=0: beta=10.0`.

        _BOUNDS_CACHE[n]=b
    return b
```

`run_lbfgs_direct`:
```
def run_lbfgs_direct(a0,beta,deadline,maxfun):
    a=normalize_a(a0)
    n=len(a)
    if n==0: return inf,a
    sc0=exact_score_a(a)
    if deadline is not None:
       rem=float(deadline - time.monotonic())
       if rem < 8: return sc0,a
       cap=int((0.8*rem)/max(estimate_eval_time(n),1e-4))
       maxfun=int(min(maxfun, cap))
    if maxfun<50: return sc0,a
    opts={'ftol':1e-12,'gtol':1e-10,'maxiter':max(30,maxfun//2),'maxfun':maxfun,'maxls':20}
    try:
       res=minimize(lse_direct_fn,a,method='L-BFGS-B',jac=True,args=(float(beta),PEN_DIRECT),bounds=get_bounds(n),options=opts)
    except Exception:
       return sc0,a
    if getattr(res,'x',None) is not None:
       a_out=normalize_a(res.x)
       sc=exact_score_a(a_out)
       if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < 1e8:
           return float(sc), a_out
    return sc0,a
```
If min returns worse due LSE, exact may be worse; return anyway; optimize_sequence decides.

`softmax_from_x`:
```
def softmax_from_x(x):
  x=np.nan_to_num(np.asarray(x,float), nan=0.0, posinf=50.0, neginf=-50.0)
  if x.size==0: return empty
  x=x - float(np.mean(x))
  # if variance huge, clip to [-40,40]
  mx=float(np.max(x)); mn=float(np.min(x))
  if not np.isfinite(mx) or not np.isfinite(mn): x=np.zeros_like(x)
  if mx-mn > 60.0:
      x=np.clip(x, -40.0, 40.0)
  z=x - np.max(x)
  z=np.clip(z,-745.0,0.0)
  v=np.exp(z); s=float(v.sum())
  if s<1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s): return np.full(x.size,1.0/x.size)
  p=v/s
  return p
```
Maybe clipping to [-40,40] changes mean; okay. Uniform if flat.

`log_from_a`:
```
def log_from_a(a):
 a=normalize_a(a); n=a.size
 if n==0: return empty
 eps = max(1e-15, 1e-12/float(n))
 a = np.maximum(a, eps)
 x=np.log(a)
 x = x - float(np.mean(x))
 return x
```

`lse_log_fn` details:
```
def lse_log_fn(x,beta,n,reg):
    n=int(n)
    x=np.nan_to_num(np.asarray(x,float).ravel(), nan=0.0, posinf=50.0, neginf=-50.0)
    grad_x=np.zeros(n)
    if x.shape[0]!=n: # maybe pad/trunc
       if x.size<n: x=np.pad(x,(0,n-x.size)); else x=x[:n]
    x = x - float(np.mean(x))
    if float(np.max(np.abs(x))) > 60.0:
       x = np.clip(x,-40.0,40.0)
    z=x-np.max(x); z=np.clip(z,-745,0)
    v=np.exp(z); s=float(v.sum())
    if s<=0 or not finite: return 1e100, grad_x
    p=v/s
    c=fast_self_conv(p)
    cmax=float(np.max(c))
    if cmax <= 1e-300: return 1e100, grad_x
    beta=float(beta); if not finite or beta<=0: beta=100.0
    beff=beta*float(n)
    zc=beff*(c-cmax); zc=np.clip(zc,-745,0)
    e=np.exp(zc); sum_e=float(e.sum()); if sum_e<=0...
    w=e/sum_e
    L=float(n)*cmax + np.log(sum_e)/beta
    h=corr_pair(p,w)
    lds_c=float(np.dot(w,c))
    grad_p=4.0*float(n)*(h - lds_c)
    grad_p=np.nan_to_num(grad_p,...); grad_p=np.clip(grad_p,-1e6,1e6)
    grad_x = p*grad_p
    grad_x -= float(np.mean(grad_x))
    val = float(n)*(2.0*L)
    if reg>0:
       val += float(n)*reg*float(np.dot(x,x))
       grad_x = grad_x*float(n) + float(n)*2.0*reg*x
    else:
       grad_x = grad_x*float(n)
    # If gradient huge, clip
    grad_x=np.nan_to_num(grad_x); grad_x=np.clip(grad_x,-1e4,1e4)
    if not np.isfinite(val): return 1e100, zeros
    return float(val), grad_x
```
Potential: `np.dot(x,x)` after mean subtraction; if x huge, reg val huge, but x clipped. Good.
Scaling gradient by n after mean removal. If n=32768, grad_x main O? grad_p~4n(h-l) O? h,l~1/n, diff~1/n =>4n*1/n=O1, p~1/n =>grad_x pre-scale~1/n, scale n =>O1. Good.
Reg gradient n*2reg*x, with reg=1e-10 -> O(0.006) for n32768; okay.

`run_lbfgs_log_from_a`:
```
def run_lbfgs_log_from_a(a0,beta,deadline,maxfun):
    a=normalize_a(a0); n=len(a)
    sc0=exact_score_a(a)
    if n==0 return
    if deadline: rem... cap
    if maxfun<50 return
    x=log_from_a(a)
    opts={'ftol':1e-10,'gtol':1e-7,'maxiter':max(30,maxfun//2),'maxfun':maxfun,'maxls':20}
    try: res=minimize(lse_log_fn,x,method='L-BFGS-B',jac=True,args=(float(beta),n,1e-10),options=opts)
    except: return sc0,a
    if res.x is not None:
       a_out=softmax_from_x(res.x)
       sc=exact_score_a(a_out)
       if finite and sc<1e8: return sc,a_out
    return sc0,a
```
No bounds. If `minimize` fails due nan, returns initial.

`active_weights`:
```
def active_weights(c,k):
 c=np.asarray(c,float); m=c.size
 if m==0: return empty
 k=int(k)
 if k<=0 or k>=m: return np.ones(m)/m
 idx=np.argpartition(c,m-k)[m-k:]
 w=np.zeros(m); w[idx]=1.0/float(k)
 return w
```
If tie at max, argpartition arbitrary.

`subgrad_polish`:
```
def subgrad_polish(a,max_iters,deadline):
 a=normalize_a(a); best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=exact_score_a(a); cur_a=best_a.copy(); cur_sc=best_sc; n=len(a)
 max_iters=max(1,int(max_iters))
 for it in range(max_iters):
   if deadline and time>deadline: break
   c=fast_self_conv(a); S=float(a.sum())
   if c.size==0 or S<1e-12: break
   cmax=float(c.max()); if cmax<=0: break
   cur=None; cur_sc_new=cur_sc
   # use c as is (unnormalized), score invariant; weights not matter
   for k in (1,4,8):
     if k > c.size: continue
     w=active_weights(c,k)
     h=corr_pair(a,w)
     hmax=float(h.max())
     if hmax < 1e-300: continue
     for mult in (0.01,0.04,0.12,0.3,0.6):
        if deadline and time>deadline: break
        eta=mult/hmax
        q=a*np.exp(-eta*h)
        q=normalize_a(q)
        sc=exact_score_a(q)
        if not finite: continue
        if sc<best_sc: best_sc=sc; best_a=q.copy()
        if sc<cur_sc_new: cur_sc_new=sc; cur=q
   if cur is None: break
   if cur_sc_new < cur_sc:
      cur_a=cur; cur_sc=cur_sc_new
   else:
      break
   a=cur_a
 # final best
 return best_sc,best_a
```
Could use `c` normalized? Since score invariant, top same. Good.
Note `hmax` could be small for k large; eta large but eta*hmax=mult. safe.

`smooth_noise` and `perturb_best` as previous. We'll include.
`smooth_noise` uses np.convolve with kernel; for n large okay.
`perturb_best` modes 0-5. Use `np.exp(strength*rng.standard_normal(n))`; if under/overflow? strength<=0.8, standard normal 5 -> exp4 safe. normalize.
Mode 3 pressure: `h=corr_pair(p, active_weights(c,4))`? Need c. We can compute c=fast_self_conv(p); w=active_weights(c,4); h=corr_pair(p,w). hmax. q=p*exp(-strength*h/hmax). Good.

`add_init`:
```
def add_init(inits, arr, cap, min_val=1e-8):
 if len(inits)>=cap: return
 arr=np.asarray(arr,float).ravel()
 if arr.size==0: return
 arr=np.nan_to_num(arr,nan=0.0,posinf=1e3,neginf=0.0)
 mn=float(np.min(arr))
 if mn < min_val: arr=arr - mn + min_val if mn< min_val else arr? Actually if mn<0, shift; if mn=0 and we need positive for log, add min_val.
 arr=np.maximum(arr, min_val)
 inits.append(normalize_a(arr))
```
If we add min_val to all, changes shape slightly. Good for init.

`generate_inits` details:
Need `x` if n=1. Use n maybe >1024. For n=1, handle? Not needed but robust.
```
def generate_inits(n,parent,rng):
 n=int(n); cap=16 if n>16384 else 24
 inits=[]
 x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
 if parent is not None and parent.size>0:
    pr=resample_sequence(parent,n)
    add_init(inits,pr,cap,1e-8)
    add_init(inits,pr[::-1],cap,1e-8)
 add_init(inits, np.ones(n), cap)
 for alpha in (0.2,0.5,0.8):
    add_init(inits, 1.0+alpha*(2x-1), cap)
    add_init(inits, 1.0-alpha*(2x-1), cap)
 for pwr in (0.5,2.0,3.0):
    add_init(inits, x**pwr, cap)
    add_init(inits, (1-x)**pwr, cap)
 max_trig=6 if n>16384 else 10
 for r in range(max_trig):
    vals=np.ones(n)
    K=int(rng.integers(1,4))
    for _ in range(K):
       f=int(rng.integers(1, max(2, n//10)))
       amp=float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.35))
       ph=float(rng.uniform(-np.pi,np.pi))
       vals += amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*f*x + ph)
       if r%2==1: vals += 0.5*amp*np.sin(...)
    add_init(inits, vals, cap)
 max_low=3 if n>16384 else5
 Nf=next_power_of_two(n)
 for _ in range(max_low):
    freqs=np.fft.rfftfreq(Nf)*Nf
    power=1.0/((np.abs(freqs)+1.0)**float(rng.uniform(1.5,3.0)))
    power[0]=0.0
    phase=rng.uniform(-np.pi,np.pi,size=len(power))
    spec=power*np.exp(1j*phase); spec[0]=0.0
    noise=np.fft.irfft(spec,Nf)[:n]
    noise=noise-noise.mean()
    vals=1.0+float(rng.uniform(0.3,0.8))*noise
    add_init(inits, vals, cap)
 for nb in (8,16,32):
    if nb>n: nb=n
    block_vals=0.1+0.9*rng.random(nb)
    per=max(1,n//nb)
    a=np.repeat(block_vals,per)
    if a.size<n: a=np.concatenate([a,np.repeat(block_vals,n-a.size)])
    add_init(inits,a[:n],cap)
 for _ in range(3):
    add_init(inits, 0.05+0.95*rng.random(n), cap)
 return inits
```

Let's code `optimize_sequence` carefully:
```
def optimize_sequence(a0, deadline, rng, label, kind):
    a=normalize_a(a0)
    if a.size==0: return np.inf,a
    best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=exact_score_a(a)
    n=len(a)
    t_start=time.monotonic()
    total=max(10.0, float(deadline - t_start))
    print("[{}] start score={:.8f} n={} kind={}".format(label,best_sc,n,kind))
    schedule = [(20.0,0.05),(60.0,0.06),(150.0,0.08),(400.0,0.10),(1000.0,0.12),(2500.0,0.14),(6000.0,0.14),(15000.0,0.12),(40000.0,0.19)]
    cum=0.0
    no_improve_streak=0
    for beta,frac in schedule:
        if time.monotonic() >= float(deadline) - 20.0: break
        sub_dead = t_start + (cum + frac) * total
        sub_dead = min(float(sub_dead), float(deadline) - 5.0)
        cum += frac
        if sub_dead <= time.monotonic() + 8.0: continue
        attempts=0
        while time.monotonic() < sub_dead - 8.0 and attempts < 4:
            rem = float(sub_dead - time.monotonic() - 2.0)
            if rem < 10.0: break
            mf=estimate_maxfun(rem*0.75,n)
            if mf < 50: break
            try:
                if kind == 'log': sc,a_run=run_lbfgs_log_from_a(a,beta,float(sub_dead),mf)
                else: sc,a_run=run_lbfgs_direct(a,beta,float(sub_dead),mf)
            except Exception:
                sc=exact_score_a(a); a_run=a
            attempts+=1
            if not np.isfinite(sc): break
            if sc < best_sc:
                best_sc=sc; best_a=a_run.copy(); a=a_run.copy(); no_improve_streak=0
                print("[{}] new best {:.8f} beta={:.0f} elapsed={:.0f}".format(label,best_sc,beta,time.monotonic()-t_start))
            else:
                no_improve_streak += 1
                if sc < best_sc + 0.003:
                    a = a_run.copy()
                # else keep a
            if sc >= best_sc + 0.01:
                # run made things worse; reset to best and try next beta? set a=best_a and break this beta
                a = best_a.copy()
                break
            if attempts >= 3 and sc >= best_sc:
                break
    if time.monotonic() < float(deadline) - 30.0:
        # final high beta few iterations using kind
        rem=float(deadline - time.monotonic() - 25.0)
        if rem > 30.0:
            mf=estimate_maxfun(rem*0.7,n)
            try:
                if kind=='log': sc,a_run=run_lbfgs_log_from_a(a,80000.0,float(deadline),mf)
                else: sc,a_run=run_lbfgs_direct(a,80000.0,float(deadline),mf)
                if sc<best_sc: best...
            except: pass
    return float(best_sc), best_a
```

def quick_optimize_log(a, deadline):
 a=normalize_a(a); best=...; n=len(a); t_end=float(deadline)
 for beta in (1500.0,8000.0,40000.0):
   if time.monotonic() >= t_end-10: break

   if rem<20: break

   sc,a_run=run_lbfgs_log_from_a(a,beta,t_end,mf)
   if sc<best: update; a=a_run
   else: if sc<best+0.01: a=a_run
 return best,a

Potential issue: In `run_lbfgs_log_from_a`, if `res.nfev` zero due failure? res.x maybe initial. ok.

Potential issue: scipy `minimize` with `jac=True` and function returns tuple; good.

### Final code
Let's produce. (I need be careful with indentation and imports; allowed numpy, scipy, math. Use time.)
</think>

```python
"""
Search for a non-negative sequence minimizing

    2 * n * max(conv(a, a)) / sum(a)^2

using:
- FFT-based exact scoring,
- LSE-smooth max-convolution surrogates,
- both a plaintext nonnegative-variable L-BFGS-B optimizer and a
  log/softmax reparameterization to enforce positivity and normalize mass,
- multi-stage length continuation (4096/8192/16384),
- diverse initial sequences from the previous parent when available,
- exact active-set multiplicative pressure polish,
- final perturb-and-reoptimize restarts.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize


PEN_DIRECT = 1e-3
MAX_SEARCH_LEN = 16384


_BOUNDS_CACHE = {}


def next_power_of_two(m):
    m = int(max(1, m))
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def fast_self_conv(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    m = 2 * n - 1
    nfft = next_power_of_two(m)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, nfft)
    out = np.fft.irfft(A * A, nfft)[:m]
    return np.maximum(out, 0.0)


def corr_pair(a, w):
    """
    h_j = sum_k w_k * a_{k-j}, for j=0,...,n-1.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    m = int(w.size)
    if n == 0 or m == 0:
        return np.empty(max(0, n), dtype=np.float64)
    L = n + m - 1
    nfft = next_power_of_two(L)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], nfft)
    W = np.fft.rfft(w, nfft)
    full = np.fft.irfft(A * W, nfft)[:L]
    out = full[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    return np.nan_to_num(out, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)


def normalize_a(a, target=1.0):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S < 1e-12:
        return np.full(a.size, float(target) / float(a.size), dtype=np.float64)
    a = a * (float(target) / S)
    S2 = float(a.sum())
    if S2 < 1e-12:
        return np.full(a.size, float(target) / float(a.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return a * (float(target) / S2)


def resample_sequence(a, n):
    a = np.nan_to_num(
        np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel(),
        nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0
    )
    m = int(a.size)
    n = int(n)
    if m == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(max(1, n))
    if m == n:
        return a.copy()
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    out = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a).astype(np.float64)
    return np.maximum(out, 0.0)


def exact_score_a(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S < 0.01:
        return np.inf
    c = fast_self_conv(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        cmax = 0.0
    else:
        cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if cmax < 0.0:
        cmax = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * cmax / (S * S))


def d_from_a(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64), 0.0
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S < 1e-12:
        return np.zeros(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.float64), S
    c = fast_self_conv(a)
    d = (float(n) * c) / (S * S)
    return np.maximum(d, 0.0), S


def get_bounds(n):
    n = int(n)
    b = _BOUNDS_CACHE.get(n)
    if b is None:
        if len(_BOUNDS_CACHE) > 5:
            _BOUNDS_CACHE.clear()
        b = [(0.0, 10.0)] * n
        _BOUNDS_CACHE[n] = b
    return b


def estimate_eval_time(n):
    n = float(max(16, int(n)))
    logn = max(1.0, float(np.log2(max(16.0, n))))
    scale = max(0.5, n / 8192.0)
    return 0.035 * scale * max(1.0, logn / np.log2(8192.0))


def estimate_maxfun(t_left, n):
    t_left = float(max(1.0, t_left))
    if t_left < 6.0:
        return 64
    est = estimate_eval_time(n)
    mf = int((0.72 * t_left) / max(est, 1e-4))
    return max(50, min(2600, mf))


def lse_direct_fn(a, beta, pen):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 0:
        return 1e100, grad

    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1e3, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S < 1e-8:
        return 1e100, grad

    beta = float(beta)
    if not np.isfinite(beta) or beta <= 1e-6:
        beta = 50.0

    c = fast_self_conv(a)
    d = (float(n) * c) / (S * S)
    dmax = float(np.max(d))
    if not np.isfinite(dmax) or dmax <= 0.0:
        return 1e100, grad

    z = beta * (d - dmax)
    z = np.clip(z, -745.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    sum_e = float(e.sum())
    if sum_e <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(sum_e):
        return 1e100, grad
    w = e / sum_e

    h = corr_pair(a, w)
    LDS = float(np.dot(w, d))

    score = 2.0 * (dmax + np.log(sum_e) / beta) + float(pen) * (S - 1.0) * (S - 1.0)

    grad = (4.0 * float(n) * h) / (S * S)
    grad -= (4.0 * LDS) / S
    grad += 2.0 * float(pen) * (S - 1.0)

    grad = np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    grad = np.clip(grad, -1e6, 1e6)
    return float(score), grad


def run_lbfgs_direct(a0, beta, deadline, maxfun):
    a = normalize_a(a0)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf, a
    sc0 = exact_score_a(a)

    if deadline is not None:
        rem = float(deadline - time.monotonic())
        if rem < 10.0:
            return sc0, a
        cap = int((0.85 * rem) / max(estimate_eval_time(n), 1e-4))
        maxfun = int(min(int(maxfun), max(50, cap)))
    else:
        maxfun = int(max(50, maxfun))

    opts = {
        "ftol": 1e-12,
        "gtol": 1e-9,
        "maxiter": max(30, maxfun // 2),
        "maxfun": int(maxfun),
        "maxls": 20,
    }

    try:
        res = minimize(
            lse_direct_fn,
            a,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=True,
            args=(float(beta), PEN_DIRECT),
            bounds=get_bounds(n),
            options=opts,
        )
    except Exception:
        return sc0, a

    if getattr(res, "x", None) is not None:
        a_out = normalize_a(res.x)
        sc = exact_score_a(a_out)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < 1e8:
            return float(sc), a_out

    return sc0, a


def softmax_from_x(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(x.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)

    x = np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.0, posinf=50.0, neginf=-50.0)
    x = x - float(np.mean(x))

    if float(np.max(np.abs(x))) > 80.0:
        x = np.clip(x, -40.0, 40.0)

    z = x - float(np.max(x))
    z = np.clip(z, -745.0, 0.0)
    v = np.exp(z)
    s = float(v.sum())
    if s < 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    return v / s


def log_from_a(a):
    a = normalize_a(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    eps = max(1e-15, 1e-12 / float(n))
    a = np.maximum(a, eps)
    x = np.log(a)
    x -= float(np.mean(x))
    return x


def lse_log_fn(x, beta, n, reg):
    n = int(n)
    grad_x = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 0:
        return 1e100, grad_x

    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if x.size < n:
        x = np.pad(x, (0, n - x.size), mode="constant")
    elif x.size > n:
        x = x[:n]

    x = np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.0, posinf=50.0, neginf=-50.0)
    x = x - float(np.mean(x))
    if float(np.max(np.abs(x))) > 80.0:
        x = np.clip(x, -40.0, 40.0)

    z = x - float(np.max(x))
    z = np.clip(z, -745.0, 0.0)
    v = np.exp(z)
    s = float(v.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return 1e100, grad_x
    p = v / s

    c = fast_self_conv(p)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if cmax <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(cmax):
        return 1e100, grad_x

    beta = float(beta)
    if not np.isfinite(beta) or beta <= 1e-6:
        beta = 50.0

    beff = beta * float(n)
    zc = beff * (c - cmax)
    zc = np.clip(zc, -745.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(zc)
    sum_e = float(e.sum())
    if sum_e <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(sum_e):
        return 1e100, grad_x
    w = e / sum_e

    L = float(n) * cmax + np.log(sum_e) / beta
    h = corr_pair(p, w)
    lds_c = float(np.dot(w, c))

    # p is already normalized. The projected gradient for the exact score
    # is 4n * (corr(p,w) - E_w[c]).
    grad_p = 4.0 * float(n) * (h - lds_c)
    grad_p = np.nan_to_num(grad_p, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    grad_p = np.clip(grad_p, -1e6, 1e6)

    grad_x = p * grad_p
    grad_x -= float(np.mean(grad_x))

    val = float(n) * (2.0 * L)
    if reg > 0.0:
        xr = x - float(np.mean(x))
        val += float(n) * float(reg) * float(np.dot(xr, xr))
        grad_x = grad_x * float(n) + float(n) * 2.0 * float(reg) * xr
    else:
        grad_x = grad_x * float(n)

    grad_x = np.nan_to_num(grad_x, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    grad_x = np.clip(grad_x, -1e4, 1e4)

    if not np.isfinite(val):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return float(val), grad_x


def run_lbfgs_log_from_a(a0, beta, deadline, maxfun):
    a = normalize_a(a0)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf, a
    sc0 = exact_score_a(a)

    if deadline is not None:
        rem = float(deadline - time.monotonic())
        if rem < 10.0:
            return sc0, a
        cap = int((0.85 * rem) / max(estimate_eval_time(n), 1e-4))
        maxfun = int(min(int(maxfun), max(50, cap)))
    else:
        maxfun = int(max(50, maxfun))

    x0 = log_from_a(a)

    opts = {
        "ftol": 1e-10,
        "gtol": 1e-7,
        "maxiter": max(30, maxfun // 2),
        "maxfun": int(maxfun),
        "maxls": 20,
    }

    try:
        res = minimize(
            lse_log_fn,
            x0,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=True,
            args=(float(beta), int(n), 1e-10),
            options=opts,
        )
    except Exception:
        return sc0, a

    if getattr(res, "x", None) is not None:
        a_out = softmax_from_x(res.x)
        sc = exact_score_a(a_out)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < 1e8:
            return float(sc), a_out

    return sc0, a


def active_weights(c, k):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    m = int(c.size)
    if m == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    k = int(k)
    if k <= 0 or k >= m:
        return np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)
    idx = np.argpartition(c, m - k)[m - k:]
    w = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
    w[idx] = 1.0 / float(k)
    return w


def subgrad_polish(a, max_iters, deadline):
    a = normalize_a(a)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.inf, a

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = exact_score_a(a)
    cur_a = best_a.copy()
    cur_sc = best_sc
    n = len(a)

    max_iters = max(1, int(max_iters))
    ks = (1, 4, 8, 16)
    mults = (0.01, 0.04, 0.10, 0.25, 0.55)

    for _ in range(max_iters):
        if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > float(deadline):
            break
        c = fast_self_conv(a)
        if c.size == 0:
            break
        cmax = float(np.max(c))
        if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= 0.0:
            break

        cur = None
        cur_new_sc = cur_sc

        for k in ks:
            if k > c.size:
                continue
            w = active_weights(c, k)
            h = corr_pair(a, w)
            hmax = float(np.max(h))
            if hmax < 1e-300:
                continue
            for mult in mults:
                if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > float(deadline):
                    break
                eta = float(mult) / hmax
                q = a * np.exp(-eta * h)
                q = normalize_a(q)
                sc = exact_score_a(q)
                if not np.isfinite(sc):
                    continue
                if sc < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc
                    best_a = q.copy()
                if sc < cur_new_sc:
                    cur_new_sc = sc
                    cur = q

        if cur is None or cur_new_sc >= cur_sc:
            break
        cur_sc = cur_new_sc
        cur_a = cur
        a = cur_a

    return float(best_sc), best_a


def smooth_noise(n, rng, strength):
    n = int(n)
    noise = rng.standard_normal(n)
    k = min(31, max(3, n // 200))
    if k % 2 == 0:
        k += 1
    if k > n:
        k = n if n % 2 == 1 else max(3, n - 1)
    if k >= 3:
        c = k // 2
        sig = max(1.0, c / 2.0)
        kern = np.exp(-((np.arange(k) - c) ** 2) / (2.0 * sig * sig))
        kern = kern / float(kern.sum())
        noise = np.convolve(noise, kern, mode="same")
    noise = noise - float(noise.mean())
    return noise * float(strength)


def perturb_best(a, rng, strength=None):
    p = normalize_a(a)
    n = int(p.size)
    if n == 0:
        return p
    if strength is None:
        strength = float(rng.uniform(0.12, 0.65))
    mode = int(rng.integers(0, 6))

    if mode == 0:
        q = p * np.exp(strength * rng.standard_normal(n))
    elif mode == 1:
        t = float(rng.uniform(0.10, 0.45))
        q = (1.0 - t) * p + t * (np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n))
    elif mode == 2:
        q = p[::-1].copy()
        q = q * np.exp(0.10 * rng.standard_normal(n))
    elif mode == 3:
        c = fast_self_conv(p)
        w = active_weights(c, 4)
        h = corr_pair(p, w)
        hmax = float(np.max(h))
        if hmax < 1e-300:
            q = p.copy()
        else:
            q = p * np.exp(-strength * h / hmax)
    elif mode == 4:
        logp = np.log(p + 1e-300)
        logp = logp + smooth_noise(n, rng, strength * 0.5)
        q = np.exp(logp - float(np.max(logp)))
    else:
        k = int(rng.integers(3, 15))
        if k % 2 == 0:
            k += 1
        if k > n:
            k = n if n % 2 == 1 else max(3, n - 1)
        if k < 3:
            k = 3
        c = k // 2
        sig = max(1.0, c / 2.0)
        kern = np.exp(-((np.arange(k) - c) ** 2) / (2.0 * sig * sig))
        knorm = float(kern.sum())
        if knorm > 0.0:
            kern = kern / knorm
        q = np.convolve(p, kern, mode="same")

    return normalize_a(q)


def add_init(inits, arr, cap, min_val=1e-8):
    if len(inits) >= cap:
        return
    arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return
    arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1e3, neginf=0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, float(min_val))
    inits.append(normalize_a(arr))


def generate_inits(n, rng, parent, cap=None):
    n = int(n)
    if cap is None:
        cap = 16 if n > 8192 else 24
    inits = []
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)

    if parent is not None and int(parent.size) > 0:
        pr = resample_sequence(parent, n)
        add_init(inits, pr, cap)
        add_init(inits, pr[::-1], cap)

    add_init(inits, np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), cap)

    for alpha in (0.2, 0.5, 0.8):
        add_init(inits, 1.0 + alpha * (2.0 * x - 1.0), cap)
        add_init(inits, 1.0 - alpha * (2.0 * x - 1.0), cap)

    for pwr in (0.5, 2.0, 3.0):
        add_init(inits, x ** pwr, cap)
        add_init(inits, (1.0 - x) ** pwr, cap)

    max_trig = 6 if n > 8192 else 10
    for r in range(max_trig):
        vals = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        K = int(rng.integers(1, 4))
        for _ in range(K):
            f = int(rng.integers(1, max(2, n // 10)))
            amp = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.35))
            ph = float(rng.uniform(-np.pi, np.pi))
            vals += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f * x + ph)
            if r % 2 == 1:
                vals += 0.5 * amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * f * x + ph)
        add_init(inits, vals, cap)

    max_low = 3 if n > 8192 else 5
    Nf = next_power_of_two(n)
    for _ in range(max_low):
        freqs = np.fft.rfftfreq(Nf) * Nf
        power = 1.0 / ((np.abs(freqs) + 1.0) ** float(rng.uniform(1.5, 3.0)))
        power[0] = 0.0
        phase = rng.uniform(-np.pi, np.pi, size=len(power))
        spec = power * np.exp(1j * phase)
        spec[0] = 0.0
        noise = np.fft.irfft(spec, Nf)[:n]
        noise = noise - float(noise.mean())
        vals = 1.0 + float(rng.uniform(0.3, 0.8)) * noise
        add_init(inits, vals, cap)

    for nb in (8, 16, 32):
        if nb > n:
            nb = n
        block_vals = 0.1 + 0.9 * rng.random(nb)
        per = max(1, n // nb)
        a = np.repeat(block_vals, per)
        if a.size < n:
            a = np.concatenate([a, np.repeat(block_vals, n - a.size)])
        add_init(inits, a[:n], cap)

    for _ in range(3):
        add_init(inits, 0.05 + 0.95 * rng.random(n), cap)

    return inits


def make_initial(n, parent, rng):
    n = int(n)
    inits = generate_inits(n, rng, parent)
    best_a = None
    best_sc = np.inf
    for arr in inits:
        a = normalize_a(arr)
        sc = exact_score_a(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a.copy()
    if best_a is None:
        best_a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        best_sc = exact_score_a(best_a)
    return float(best_sc), best_a


def optimize_sequence(a0, deadline, rng, label, kind):
    a = normalize_a(a0)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.inf, a

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = exact_score_a(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    total = max(10.0, float(deadline - t_start))

    print("[{}] start score={:.8f} n={} kind={}".format(label, best_sc, n, kind))

    schedule = [
        (20.0, 0.05),
        (60.0, 0.06),
        (150.0, 0.08),
        (400.0, 0.10),
        (1000.0, 0.12),
        (2500.0, 0.14),
        (6000.0, 0.14),
        (15000.0, 0.12),
        (40000.0, 0.19),
    ]

    cum = 0.0
    for beta, frac in schedule:
        if time.monotonic() >= float(deadline) - 20.0:
            break
        sub_dead = t_start + (cum + frac) * total
        sub_dead = min(float(sub_dead), float(deadline) - 5.0)
        cum += frac
        if sub_dead <= time.monotonic() + 10.0:
            continue

        attempts = 0
        while time.monotonic() < sub_dead - 8.0 and attempts < 4:
            rem = float(sub_dead - time.monotonic() - 2.0)
            if rem < 12.0:
                break
            mf = estimate_maxfun(rem * 0.75, n)
            if mf < 50:
                break

            try:
                if kind == "log":
                    sc, a_run = run_lbfgs_log_from_a(a, beta, float(sub_dead), mf)
                else:
                    sc, a_run = run_lbfgs_direct(a, beta, float(sub_dead), mf)
            except Exception:
                sc = exact_score_a(a)
                a_run = a

            attempts += 1
            if not np.isfinite(sc):
                break

            if sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best_a = a_run.copy()
                a = a_run.copy()
                print(
                    "[{}] new best {:.8f} beta={:.0f} elapsed={:.0f}s".format(
                        label, best_sc, beta, time.monotonic() - t_start
                    )
                )
            else:
                if sc < best_sc + 0.003:
                    a = a_run.copy()
                if sc >= best_sc + 0.01:
                    a = best_a.copy()
                    break
                if attempts >= 3 and sc >= best_sc:
                    break

    if time.monotonic() < float(deadline) - 30.0:
        rem = float(deadline - time.monotonic() - 25.0)
        if rem > 30.0:
            mf = estimate_maxfun(rem * 0.7, n)
            try:
                if kind == "log":
                    sc, a_run = run_lbfgs_log_from_a(a, 60000.0, float(deadline), mf)
                else:
                    sc, a_run = run_lbfgs_direct(a, 60000.0, float(deadline), mf)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc
                    best_a = a_run.copy()
                    print("[{}] final high-beta best {:.8f}".format(label, best_sc))
            except Exception:
                pass

    return float(best_sc), best_a


def quick_optimize_log(a, deadline):
    a = normalize_a(a)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.inf, a
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = exact_score_a(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    t_end = float(deadline)

    for beta in (1500.0, 8000.0, 40000.0):
        if time.monotonic() >= t_end - 12.0:
            break
        rem = t_end - time.monotonic() - 4.0
        if rem < 20.0:
            break
        mf = estimate_maxfun(rem * 0.7, n)
        if mf < 50:
            break
        try:
            sc, a_run = run_lbfgs_log_from_a(a, beta, t_end, mf)
        except Exception:
            break
        if not np.isfinite(sc):
            continue
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a_run.copy()
            a = a_run.copy()
        else:
            if sc < best_sc + 0.005:
                a = a_run.copy()
            else:
                a = best_a.copy()

    return float(best_sc), best_a


def get_parent():
    try:
        h = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    except Exception:
        return None
    if h is None:
        return None
    try:
        p = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        p = np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0.0, posinf=1e3, neginf=0.0)
        p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if p.size < 32:
            return None
        return normalize_a(p)
    except Exception:
        return None


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    total_budget = float(budget_s)
    hard_deadline = t_start + max(120.0, total_budget - 12.0)
    stage_end = hard_deadline - 160.0
    usable = max(60.0, stage_end - t_start)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    parent = get_parent()

    best_a = None
    best_sc = np.inf

    def update_if_better(sc, a):
        nonlocal best_a, best_sc
        if a is None:
            return
        a = normalize_a(a)
        sc = float(sc)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a.copy()

    if parent is None:
        stages = [
            (4096, 0.08, "direct", "A4k"),
            (8192, 0.17, "direct", "B8k"),
            (16384, 0.43, "direct", "C16k-direct"),
            (16384, 0.22, "log", "D16k-log"),
        ]
    else:
        stages = [
            (8192, 0.12, "direct", "A8k-direct"),
            (16384, 0.43, "direct", "B16k-direct"),
            (16384, 0.33, "log", "C16k-log"),
        ]

    cum = 0.0
    for stage_idx, (n_len, frac, kind, label) in enumerate(stages):
        n_len = int(min(MAX_SEARCH_LEN, max(1024, n_len)))
        sub_dead = t_start + (cum + frac) * usable
        cum += frac
        sub_dead = min(float(sub_dead), float(stage_end))

        if time.monotonic() > sub_dead - 40.0:
            continue

        if stage_idx == 0 or best_a is None:
            if parent is not None and n_len < int(parent.size):
                sc_init, a_init = make_initial(n_len, parent, rng)
            elif parent is not None:
                a_init = normalize_a(resample_sequence(parent, n_len))
                sc_init = exact_score_a(a_init)
            else:
                sc_init, a_init = make_initial(n_len, None, rng)
            update_if_better(sc_init, a_init)
        else:
            base = best_a if best_a is not None else (normalize_a(resample_sequence(parent, n_len)) if parent is not None else np.ones(4096, dtype=np.float64) / 4096.0)
            if len(base) != n_len:
                a0 = normalize_a(resample_sequence(base, n_len))
            elif kind == "log":
                a0 = perturb_best(base, rng, 0.30)
            else:
                a0 = perturb_best(base, rng, 0.18)
            sc0 = exact_score_a(a0)
            update_if_better(sc0, a0)

        if time.monotonic() > sub_dead - 40.0:
            continue

        try:
            sc, out_a = optimize_sequence(a0, sub_dead, rng, label, kind)
        except Exception:
            sc, out_a = sc0, a0

        update_if_better(sc, out_a)

        # If we have time, and this was a direct path, start the same length
        # from a perturbed best into the next stage only if explicitly log.
        if kind == "log" and time.monotonic() < sub_dead - 30.0 and best_a is not None:
            p0 = perturb_best(best_a, rng, 0.45)
            if len(p0) != n_len:
                p0 = normalize_a(resample_sequence(p0, n_len))
            try:
                _, p0 = optimize_sequence(p0, sub_dead, rng, label + "-restart", "log")
                update_if_better(exact_score_a(p0), p0)
            except Exception:
                pass

    if best_a is None:
        if parent is not None:
            best_a = normalize_a(resample_sequence(parent, min(MAX_SEARCH_LEN, max(1024, int(parent.size)))))
        else:
            best_a = np.ones(4096, dtype=np.float64) / 4096.0
        best_sc = exact_score_a(best_a)

    best_a = normalize_a(best_a)

    # Final exact active-set pressure polish.
    if time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 90.0:
        maxit = 10 if int(best_a.size) > 8192 else 20
        sc, a = subgrad_polish(best_a, max_iters=maxit, deadline=hard_deadline - 70.0)
        update_if_better(sc, a)

    # Final perturb + short log restart.
    if time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 50.0:
        p = perturb_best(best_a, rng, 0.25)
        if len(p) != int(best_a.size):
            p = normalize_a(resample_sequence(p, int(best_a.size)))
        try:
            sc, a = quick_optimize_log(p, hard_deadline - 35.0)
            update_if_better(sc, a)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Very final high-temperature run on the best sequence.
    if time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 10.0:
        rem = hard_deadline - 5.0 - time.monotonic()
        if rem > 15.0:
            n = int(best_a.size)
            mf = estimate_maxfun(rem * 0.7, n)
            if n <= 16384:
                try:
                    sc, a = run_lbfgs_direct(best_a, 80000.0, hard_deadline - 5.0, mf)
                    update_if_better(sc, a)
                except Exception:
                    pass
            else:
                try:
                    sc, a = run_lbfgs_log_from_a(best_a, 80000.0, hard_deadline - 5.0, mf)
                    update_if_better(sc, a)
                except Exception:
                    pass

    out_arr = np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    out_arr = np.nan_to_num(out_arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    out_arr = np.clip(out_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    S = float(out_arr.sum())
    if S < 0.01:
        out_arr = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) * 0.5
    else:
        out_arr = out_arr / S
        out_arr = np.clip(out_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)

    out = [float(x) for x in out_arr]
    if sum(out) < 0.01:
        out = [0.5] * 64

    try:
        verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
    except Exception:
        verified = float(best_sc)

    print(
        "FINAL internal best={:.8f} verified={:.8f} length={} time={:.1f}s".format(
            best_sc, verified, len(out), time.monotonic() - t_start
        )
    )
    return out
```
